# LU

# Lung Meridian

![image](image_001.png)

<table>
<tr><th>1</th><th></th><th>The origin of the name</th><th></th></tr>
<tr><td>hand</td><td>手</td><td>the upper limb</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>taiyin</td><td>太陰</td><td></td><td>inside of the anterior aspect of the human body</td></tr>
<tr><td>lung</td><td>肺</td><td>the lung related meridian</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>2</td><td></td><td>The course of the LU meridian</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td></td><td>1. The LU meridian originates from the lung</td><td>pectoralis major</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>and comes out through the axillary,</td><td></td><td>pectoralis minor</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="4">biceps brachii</td></tr>
</table>

2. and it goes down along the inside
of the anterior aspect of the upper and lower arm,

brachialis

brachioradialis

3. and it passes through the pulse
of the radial artery on the wrist,

pronator quadratus

thenar muscles

┌ opponens pollicis

├ abductor pollicis brevis

└ flexor pollicis brevis

4. and it is revealed at the tip of the thumb.

### 3	The acupoints of the LU meridian

anterior axillary fold 0

<tr><th>3</th><th>LU3</th></tr>

<tr><td colspan="2">8</td></tr>

<tr><td colspan="2">4</td></tr>
<tr><td>5</td><td>LU6</td></tr>

<table>
<tr><th>LU1</th><th colspan="2">중부 Jungbu 中府 Zhongfu</th><th></th></tr>
<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">On the anterior thoracic region, at the same level as the 1st<br>intercostal space, lateral to the infraclavicular fossa, 6 cun lateral<br>to the anterior midline<br>• at the same level as the 2nd intercostal space</td></tr>
<tr><td>Guide</td><td colspan="3">After locating LU2, LU1 is located 1 cun inferior to LU2.</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">pectoralis major<br>pectoralis minor<br>short head of the biceps brachii<br>coracobrachialis</td></tr>
<tr><td>nerve</td><td colspan="2">brachial plexus<br>lateral pectoral nerve<br>long thoracic nerve<br>lateral cutaneous branch of the intercostal nerve</td></tr>
<tr><td>blood<br>vessel</td><td colspan="2">cephalic vein<br>thoracoacromial artery and vein<br>axillary artery and vein</td></tr>
<tr><td>Method</td><td colspan="3">angle depth moxibustion ○<br>Caution Puncture towards the superolateral side of the<br>thoracic cage in case of the damage of the lung.</td></tr>
<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">disease of the thorax and lung</td></tr>
<tr><td>LU2</td><td colspan="2">운문 Unmun 雲門 Yunmen</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">On the anterior thoracic region, in the depression of the<br>infraclavicular fossa, medial to the coracoid process of the scapula,<br>6 cun lateral to the anterior midline<br>• at the same level as the 1st intercostal space</td></tr>
<tr><td>Guide</td><td colspan="3">LU2 is in the center of the deltopectoral triangle, which is<br>identified with the arm slightly flexed and abducted against<br>resistance.</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">platysma<br>pectoralis major<br>subclavius</td></tr>
<tr><td>nerve</td><td colspan="2">supraclavicular nerve<br>brachial plexus<br>lateral pectoral nerve<br>long thoracic nerve</td></tr>
<tr><td>blood<br>vessel</td><td colspan="2">cephalic vein<br>thoracoacromial artery and vein<br>axillary artery and vein</td></tr>
<tr><td>Method</td><td colspan="3">angle depth moxibustion ○<br>Caution Do not puncture deeply<br>towards the medial side of the thoracic cage.</td></tr>
<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">disease of the thorax and lung</td></tr>
<tr><td>LU3</td><td colspan="2">천부 Cheonbu 天府 Tianfu</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">On the anterolateral aspect of the arm, just lateral to the lateral<br>border of the biceps brachii, 3 cun inferior to the anterior axillary<br>fold</td></tr>
<tr><td>Guide</td><td colspan="3">On the line connecting the anterior axillary fold and LU5, LU3 is<br>located at the junction of the proximal 1/3 and the distal 2/3.</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">deltoid<br>pectoralis major<br>long head of the biceps brachii</td></tr>
<tr><td>nerve</td><td colspan="2">superior lateral cutaneous nerve<br>musculocutaneous nerve<br>posterior antebrachial cutaneous nerve</td></tr>
<tr><td>blood<br>vessel</td><td colspan="2">cephalic vein</td></tr>
<tr><td>Method</td><td colspan="3">angle depth moxibustion ○<br>Caution No direct moxibustion<br>to avoid the damage of the superficial blood vessels</td></tr>
<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">disease of the nose and lung</td></tr>
<tr><td>LU4</td><td colspan="2">협백 Hyeopbaek 俠白 Xiabai</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">On the anterolateral aspect of the arm, just lateral to the lateral<br>border of the biceps brachii, 4 cun inferior to the anterior axillary<br>fold</td></tr>
<tr><td>Guide</td><td colspan="3">On the line connecting the anterior axillary fold and LU5, LU4 is<br>located 1 cun distal to the junction of the proximal 1/3 and the<br>distal 2/3.</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">long head of the biceps brachii</td></tr>
<tr><td>nerve</td><td colspan="2">superior lateral cutaneous nerve<br>musculocutaneous nerve<br>posterior antebrachial cutaneous nerve</td></tr>
<tr><td>blood<br>vessel</td><td colspan="2">cephalic vein</td></tr>
<tr><td>Method</td><td colspan="3">angle depth moxibustion ○</td></tr>
<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">disease of the thorax and lung</td></tr>
<tr><td>LU5</td><td colspan="2">척택 Cheoktaek 尺澤 Chize</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">On the anterior aspect of the elbow, at the cubital crease, in the<br>depression lateral to the tendon of the biceps brachii</td></tr>
<tr><td>Guide</td><td colspan="3">With the elbow flexed, LU5 is located between LI11 and PC3 at the<br>cubital crease.</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">biceps brachii<br>brachialis<br>brachioradialis</td></tr>
<tr><td>nerve</td><td colspan="2">lateral antebrachial cutaneous nerve<br>radial nerve</td></tr>
<tr><td>blood<br>vessel</td><td colspan="2">cephalic vein<br>median cubital vein<br>radial recurrent vein</td></tr>
<tr><td>Method</td><td colspan="3">angle depth or bloodletting moxibustion ○<br>Caution No direct moxibustion</td></tr>
<tr><td>Specificity</td><td colspan="3">He-sea point</td></tr>
<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">disease of the lung and throat</td></tr>
<tr><td>LU6</td><td colspan="2">공최 Gongchoe 孔最 Kongzui</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">On the anterolateral aspect of the forearm, on the line connecting<br>LU5 and LU9, 7 cun superior to the palmar wrist crease</td></tr>
<tr><td>Guide</td><td colspan="3">LU6 is 5 cun inferior to LU5, and 1 cun proximal to the midpoint<br>of the line connecting LU5 and LU9.</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">brachioradialis<br>pronator teres<br>flexor carpi radialis<br>flexor digitorum superficialis<br>flexor pollicis longus</td></tr>
<tr><td>nerve</td><td colspan="2">lateral antebrachial cutaneous nerve<br>superficial branch of the radial nerve</td></tr>
<tr><td>blood<br>vessel</td><td colspan="2">cephalic vein<br>accessory cephalic vein<br>radial artery and vein</td></tr>
<tr><td>Method</td><td colspan="3">angle depth moxibustion ○</td></tr>
<tr><td>Specificity</td><td colspan="3">Xi-cleft point</td></tr>
<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">disease of the lung and throat</td></tr>
<tr><td>LU7</td><td colspan="2">열결 Yeolgyeol 列缺 Lieque</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">On the radial aspect of the forearm, between the tendons of the<br>abductor pollicis longus and the extensor pollicis brevis, in the<br>depression of the groove for the abductor pollicis longus tendon,<br>1.5 cun superior to the palmar wrist crease</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">tendon of the abductor pollicis longus<br>tendon of the extensor pollicis brevis<br>tendon of the brachioradialis<br>tendon of the extensor carpi radialis longus<br>pronator quadratus</td></tr>
<tr><td>nerve</td><td colspan="2">lateral antebrachial cutaneous nerve<br>superficial branch of the radial nerve</td></tr>
<tr><td>blood<br>vessel</td><td colspan="2">cephalic vein</td></tr>
<tr><td>Method</td><td colspan="3">angle or depth moxibustion ○<br>Caution Pronator quadratus is the anatomical target<br>when punctured perpendicularly.</td></tr>
<tr><td>Specificity</td><td colspan="3">Luo-connecting point, Eight confluent point(CV), Command point</td></tr>
<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">disease of the lung, throat, head and posterior neck</td></tr>
<tr><td>LU8</td><td colspan="2">경거 Gyeonggeo 經渠 Jingqu</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">On the anterolateral aspect of the forearm, between the radial<br>styloid process and the radial artery, 1 cun superior to the palmar<br>wrist crease</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">brachioradialis<br>tendon of the flexor pollicis longus<br>pronator quadratus</td></tr>
<tr><td>nerve</td><td colspan="2">lateral antebrachial cutaneous nerve<br>superficial branch of the radial nerve</td></tr>
<tr><td>blood<br>vessel</td><td colspan="2">radial artery and vein</td></tr>
<tr><td>Method</td><td colspan="3">angle or depth moxibustion ○<br>Caution No manipulation<br>to avoid the damage to the radial artery</td></tr>
<tr><td>Specificity</td><td colspan="3">Jing-river point</td></tr>
<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">disease of the heart, lung and throat</td></tr>
<tr><td>LU9</td><td colspan="2">태연 Taeyeon 太淵 Taiyuan</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">On the anterolateral aspect of the wrist, between the radial styloid<br>process and the scaphoid bone, in the depression ulnar to the<br>abductor pollicis longus tendon</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">palmar carpal ligament<br>tendon of the brachioradialis<br>tendon of the flexor carpi radialis<br>tendon of the flexor pollicis longus<br>pronator quadratus</td></tr>
<tr><td>nerve</td><td colspan="2">lateral antebrachial cutaneous nerve<br>superficial branch of the radial nerve</td></tr>
<tr><td>blood<br>vessel</td><td colspan="2">radial artery and vein</td></tr>
<tr><td>Method</td><td colspan="3">angle depth moxibustion ○<br>Caution Puncture towards the dorsal from the palmar side<br>to avoid the damage of the radial artery</td></tr>
<tr><td>Specificity</td><td colspan="3">Shu-stream point, Yuan-source point</td></tr>
<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">disease of the lung and throat</td></tr>
<tr><td>LU10</td><td colspan="2">어제 Eoje 魚際 Yuji</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">On the palm, radial to the midpoint of the 1st metacarpal bone, at<br>the border between the red and white flesh</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">abductor pollicis brevis<br>flexor pollicis brevis<br>opponens pollicis<br>tendon of the flexor pollicis longus</td></tr>
<tr><td>nerve</td><td colspan="2">common palmar digital branch of the median nerve</td></tr>
<tr><td>blood<br>vessel</td><td colspan="2">superficial palmar branch of the radial artery<br>dorsal metacarpal vein</td></tr>
<tr><td>Method</td><td colspan="3">angle depth moxibustion ○<br>Caution No direct moxibustion</td></tr>
<tr><td>Specificity</td><td colspan="3">Ying-spring point</td></tr>
<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">disease of the lung and throat</td></tr>
<tr><td>LU11</td><td colspan="2">소상 Sosang 少商 Shaoshang</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">On the thumb, radial to the distal phalanx, 0.1 cun proximal-lateral<br>to the radial corner of the thumb nail</td></tr>
<tr><td>Guide</td><td colspan="3">LU11 is at the intersection of the vertical line of the radial border<br>and the horizontal line of the base of the thumb nail.</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">nail matrix</td></tr>
<tr><td>nerve</td><td colspan="2">proper palmar digital nerve (from the median nerve)<br>dorsal digital nerve (from the radial nerve)</td></tr>
<tr><td>blood<br>vessel</td><td colspan="2">dorsal digital artery and vein</td></tr>
<tr><td>Method</td><td colspan="3">angle or depth or bloodletting moxibustion ○</td></tr>
<tr><td>Specificity</td><td colspan="3">Jing-well point</td></tr>
<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">disease of the lung and throat</td></tr>
</table>

# 手

# 陽

# 明

# 大

# 腸

# 經

# LI

# Large Intestine Meridian

![image](image_007.png)

<table>
<tr><th>1</th><th></th><th>The origin of the name</th><th></th></tr>
<tr><td>hand</td><td>手</td><td>the upper limb</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>yangming</td><td>陽明</td><td></td><td>outside of the anterior aspect of the human body</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="4">large</td></tr>
<tr><td>intestine</td><td>大腸</td><td></td><td>the large intestine related meridian</td></tr>
<tr><td>2</td><td></td><td>The course of the LI meridian</td><td></td></tr>
</table>

1. The LI meridian starts
from the tip of the index finger,

extensor pollicis longus

extensor pollicis brevis

2. and goes between the two tendons
on the wrist,

extensor carpi radialis longus

extensor carpi radialis brevis

abductor pollicis longus

between biceps and triceps brachii

3. and goes upward along the outside
of the anterior aspect

of the lower and upper arm,

4. and passes the front side
of the acromion of the scapula,

deltoid

supraspinatus

platysma

sternocleidomastoid

orbicularis oris

levator nasolabialis

levator labii superioris

5. and reaches the philtrum
through the anterior side of the neck.

### 3	The acupoints of the LI meridian

anterior axillary fold 0

<table>
<tr><th>LI1</th><th colspan="2">상양 Sangyang 商陽 Shangyang</th><th></th></tr>
<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">On the index finger, radial to the distal phalanx, 0.1 cun<br>proximal-lateral to the radial corner of the index fingernail</td></tr>
<tr><td>Guide</td><td colspan="3">LI1 is at the intersection of the vertical line of the radial border of<br>the fingernail and the horizontal line of the base of the index<br>finger.</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">nail matrix</td></tr>
<tr><td>nerve</td><td colspan="2">dorsal branch of the proper palmar digital nerve<br>(from the median nerve)</td></tr>
<tr><td>blood<br>vessel</td><td colspan="2">proper palmar digital artery<br>dorsal digital vein<br>radialis indicis artery</td></tr>
<tr><td>Method</td><td colspan="3">angle depth or bloodletting moxibustion ○</td></tr>
<tr><td>Specificity</td><td colspan="3">Jing-Well point</td></tr>
<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">disease of the mouth, teeth and throat</td></tr>
<tr><td>LI2</td><td colspan="2">이간 Igan 二間 Erjian</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">On the index finger, in the depression distal to the radial side of<br>the 2nd metacarpophalangeal joint, at the border between the red<br>and white flesh</td></tr>
<tr><td>Guide</td><td colspan="3">Make a loose fist.</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">flexor digitorum superficialis</td></tr>
<tr><td>nerve</td><td colspan="2">proper palmar digital nerve (from the median nerve)<br>dorsal digital nerve (from the radial nerve)</td></tr>
<tr><td>blood<br>vessel</td><td colspan="2">dorsal digital artery and vein<br>proper palmar digital artery<br>radialis indicis artery</td></tr>
<tr><td>Method</td><td colspan="3">angle depth moxibustion ○</td></tr>
<tr><td>Specificity</td><td colspan="3">Ying-Spring point</td></tr>
<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">disease of the mouth, teeth, throat and nose</td></tr>
<tr><td>LI3</td><td colspan="2">삼간 Samgan 三間 Sanjian</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">On the dorsum of the hand, in the depression radial and proximal<br>to the 2nd metacarpophalangeal joint</td></tr>
<tr><td>Guide</td><td colspan="3">Make a loose fist.</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">lumbrical muscle of hand<br>1st dorsal interosseous muscle of hand</td></tr>
<tr><td>nerve</td><td colspan="2">proper palmar digital nerve (from the median nerve)<br>dorsal digital nerve (from the radial nerve)</td></tr>
<tr><td>blood<br>vessel</td><td colspan="2">dorsal digital vein<br>radialis indicis artery</td></tr>
<tr><td>Method</td><td colspan="3">angle depth moxibustion ○</td></tr>
<tr><td>Specificity</td><td colspan="3">Shu-Stream point ﾠ</td></tr>
<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">disease of the mouth, teeth, throat and eyes</td></tr>
<tr><td>LI4</td><td colspan="2">합곡 Hapgok 合谷 Hegu</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">On the dorsum of the hand, radial to the midpoint of the 2nd<br>metacarpal bone</td></tr>
<tr><td>Guide</td><td colspan="3">Make the thumb abducted.</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">1st dorsal interosseous muscle<br>oblique head of the adductor pollicis muscle</td></tr>
<tr><td>nerve</td><td colspan="2">dorsal digital nerve (from the radial nerve)<br>deep branch of the ulnar nerve</td></tr>
<tr><td>blood<br>vessel</td><td colspan="2">dorsal metacarpal artery and vein<br>radialis indicis artery</td></tr>
<tr><td>Method</td><td colspan="3">angle or depth moxibustion ○<br>Caution Pregnant women especially with the experience<br>of miscarriage should be treated carefully</td></tr>
<tr><td>Specificity</td><td colspan="3">Yuan-source point, Command point</td></tr>
<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">disease of the mouth, throat and face</td></tr>
<tr><td>LI5</td><td colspan="2">양계 Yanggye 陽谿 Yangxi</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">On the postero-lateral aspect of the wrist, at the radial side of the<br>dorsal wrist crease, distal to the radial styloid process, in the<br>depression of the anatomical snuffbox</td></tr>
<tr><td>Guide</td><td colspan="3">• The depression of the anatomical snuffbox is made between the<br>tendons of the extensor pollicis longus and the extensor pollicis<br>brevis, when the thumb is fully abducted and extended.<br>• LI5 is located at the same level as SI5 and TE4.</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">extensor pollicis longus<br>extensor pollicis brevis<br>radial collateral ligament</td></tr>

# Stomach Meridian

![image](image_009.png)

<table>
<tr><th>1</th><th></th><th>The origin of the name</th><th></th></tr>
<tr><td>foot</td><td>足</td><td>the lower limb</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>yangming</td><td>陽明</td><td></td><td>outside of the anterior aspect of the human body</td></tr>
<tr><td>stomach</td><td>胃</td><td>the stomach related meridian</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>2</td><td></td><td>The course of the ST meridian</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="4">orbicularis oculi</td></tr>

1. The ST meridian starts from the nose,
passes the mouth and the mandible,

- 1) one branch goes upward to the forehead
along the anterior border of the ear,

- 2) one branch goes downward along the neck,
orbicularis oris

masseter

zygomaticus

temporalis

sternocleidomastoid

pectoralis major

<table>
<tr><th></th><th></th><th>2. and it passes through the thorax and abdomen</th><th>subclavius</th></tr>
<tr><td></td><td></td><td>from the supraclavicular fossa, and reaches the</td><td>rectus abdominis</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>inguinal canal,</td><td></td><td>external abdominis oblique inguinal ligament</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="4">sartorius</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td></td><td>3. and it reaches the knee joint from the hip joint,</td><td>rectus femoris vastus lateralis</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td></td><td>4. and it goes along the lateral side of the tibia</td><td>tibialis anterior</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>and reaches the dorsal side of the foot,</td><td></td><td>extensor digitorum longus</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>5. and it goes between the 2 nd and 3 rd toes.</td><td></td><td>extensor digitorum brevis</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="4">43</td></tr>
</table>

<tr><td>Guide</td><td colspan="3">Lateral to the angle of the mouth, ST4 is located in the nasolabial<br>sulcus or on the continuation of the nasolabial sulcus.</td></tr>

<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">On the face, anterior to the angle of the mandible, in the<br>depression anterior to the masseter attachment, over the facial<br>artery</td></tr>
<tr><td>Guide</td><td colspan="3">When the month is closed and filled with air, ST5 is located in the<br>depression, antero-inferior to the angle of the mandible.</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">platysma<br>masseter<br>medial pterygoid</td></tr>

<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">On the face, 1 cun antero-superior to the angle of the mandible</td></tr>
<tr><td>Guide</td><td colspan="3">• On the bisector of the angle of the mandible.<br>• ST6 becomes prominent with the mouth closed and the teeth<br>clenched and becomes depressed when the cleched teeth are<br>released.</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">masseter</td></tr>
<tr><td>nerve</td><td colspan="2">buccal branch of the facial nerve<br>mandibular branch of the facial nerve<br>masseteric nerve (from the mandibular nerve)</td></tr>

<tr><td>Method</td><td colspan="3">angle or depth moxibustion ○</td></tr>
<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">disease of the teeth and face</td></tr>
<tr><td>ST7</td><td colspan="2">하관 Hagwan 下關 Xiaguan</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">On the face, in the depression between the midpoint of the<br>inferior border of the zygomatic arch and the mandibular notch</td></tr>

ST31 is located in the<br>depression inferior to the apex of this triangle.<br>ST31 is located at the intersection of the line connecting the lateral<br>end of the base of the patella and the anterior superior iliac spine,<br>and the horizontal line of the inferior border of the pubic<br>symphysis.</td></tr>

# 手

# 少

# 陰

# 心

# 經

# HT

# Heart Meridian

![image](image_011.png)

| 1 |  | The origin of the name |
| --- | --- | --- |
| hand | 手 | the upper limb |
| shaoyin | 少陰 | insdie of the posterior aspect of the human body |
| heart | 心 | the heart related meridian |
| 2 |  | The course of the HT meridian |

1. The HT meridian originates from the heart
and comes out through the axillary,

coracobrachialis

2. and it goes down along the inside of the
posterior aspect of the upper arm,

short head

of the biceps brachii

3. and it keeps going along the ulnar side of the
lower arm,

flexor carpi ulnaris

4. and it is revealed at the tip of the little finger.

### 3	The acupoints of the HT meridian

<table>
<tr><th>HT1</th><th colspan="2">극천 Geukcheon 極泉 Jiquan</th><th></th></tr>
<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">In the axilla, in the center of the axillary fossa, over the axillary<br>artery</td></tr>
<tr><td>Guide</td><td colspan="3">With the elbow flexed, the arm raised and the palm behind the<br>head, HT1 is located where the axillary artery is palpated.</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">pectoralis major<br>coracobrachialis<br>subscapularis<br>teres major<br>latissimus dorsi</td></tr>
<tr><td>nerve</td><td colspan="2">intercostobrachial nerve<br>medial brachial cutaneous nerve<br>medial antebrachial cutaneous nerve<br>radial nerve<br>ulnar nerve<br>median nerve<br>axillary nerve</td></tr>
<tr><td>blood<br>vessel</td><td colspan="2">basilic vein<br>axillary artery and vein</td></tr>
<tr><td>Method</td><td colspan="3">angle depth moxibustion ○<br>Caution Avoid damage to the pleura and the lung when punctured<br>towards the thoracic cage, and the nearby blood vessels or<br>the nerves when punctured towards the upper axillary.</td></tr>
<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">disease of the heart and flank</td></tr>
<tr><td>HT2</td><td colspan="2">청령 Cheongnyeong 靑靈 Qingling</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">On the medial aspect of the arm, just medial to the medial border<br>of the biceps brachii, 3 cun superior to the cubital crease</td></tr>
<tr><td>Guide</td><td colspan="3">With the elbow flexed and the arm abducted, HT2 is located at the<br>junction of the proximal 2/3 and distal 1/3 of the line connecting<br>HT1 and HT3.</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">brachialis<br>short head of the biceps brachii</td></tr>
<tr><td>nerve</td><td colspan="2">medial antebrachial cutaneous nerve<br>ulnar nerve<br>median nerve<br>musculocutaneous nerve</td></tr>
<tr><td>blood<br>vessel</td><td colspan="2">basilic vein<br>brachial artery and vein<br>superior ulnar collateral artery</td></tr>
<tr><td>Method</td><td colspan="3">angle depth moxibustion ○</td></tr>
<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">disease of the arm and axillary</td></tr>
<tr><td>HT3</td><td colspan="2">소해 Sohae 少海 Shaohai</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">On the anteromedial aspect of the elbow, just anterior to the<br>medial epicondyle of the humerus, at the same level as the cubital<br>crease</td></tr>
<tr><td>Guide</td><td colspan="3">With the elbow flexed, HT3 is located at the midpoint of the line<br>connecting the medial end of the cubital crease and the medial<br>epicondyle of the humerus.</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">pronator teres<br>brachialis</td></tr>
<tr><td>nerve</td><td colspan="2">medial antebrachial cutaneous nerve<br>median nerve</td></tr>
<tr><td>blood<br>vessel</td><td colspan="2">basilic vein<br>brachial artery<br>inferior ulnar collateral artery<br>ulnar recurrent artery</td></tr>
<tr><td>Method</td><td colspan="3">angle depth moxibustion ○</td></tr>
<tr><td>Specificity</td><td colspan="3">He-sea point</td></tr>
<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">disease of the heart and axillary</td></tr>
<tr><td>HT4</td><td colspan="2">영도 Yeongdo 靈道 Lingdao</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">On the anteromedial aspect of the forearm, just radial to the radial<br>border of the flexor carpi ulnaris tendon, 1.5 cun proximal to the<br>palmar wrist crease</td></tr>
<tr><td>Guide</td><td colspan="3">HT4 is 1.5 cun proximal to HT7, at the same level as the proximal<br>border of the head of the ulna.</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">flexor carpi ulnaris<br>flexor digitorum superficialis<br>flexor digitorum profundus<br>pronator quadratus</td></tr>
<tr><td>nerve</td><td colspan="2">medial antebrachial cutaneous nerve<br>ulnar nerve</td></tr>
<tr><td>blood<br>vessel</td><td colspan="2">median antebrachial vein<br>ulnar artery and vein</td></tr>
<tr><td>Method</td><td colspan="3">angle depth moxibustion ○</td></tr>
<tr><td>Specificity</td><td colspan="3">Jing-river point</td></tr>
<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">disease of the heart and throat, mental illness</td></tr>
<tr><td>HT5</td><td colspan="2">통리 Tongri 通里 Tongli</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">On the anteromedial aspect of the forearm, radial to the flexor<br>carpi ulnaris tendon, 1 cun proximal to the palmar wrist crease</td></tr>
<tr><td>Guide</td><td colspan="3">HT5 is 1 cun proximal to the radial side of the proximal border of<br>the pisiform bone, 1 cun proximal to HT7, at the same level as<br>LU8.</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">flexor carpi ulnaris<br>flexor digitorum superficialis<br>flexor digitorum profundus<br>pronator quadratus</td></tr>
<tr><td>nerve</td><td colspan="2">medial antebrachial cutaneous nerve<br>ulnar nerve<br>palmar branch of the ulnar nerve</td></tr>
<tr><td>blood<br>vessel</td><td colspan="2">median antebrachial vein<br>ulnar artery and vein</td></tr>
<tr><td>Method</td><td colspan="3">angle depth moxibustion ○</td></tr>
<tr><td>Specificity</td><td colspan="3">Luo-connecting point</td></tr>
<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">disease of the heart and throat, mental illness</td></tr>
<tr><td>HT6</td><td colspan="2">음극 Eumgeuk 陰郄 Yinxi</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">On the anteromedial aspect of the forearm, radial to the flexor<br>carpi ulnaris tendon, 0.5 cun proximal to the palmar wrist crease</td></tr>
<tr><td>Guide</td><td colspan="3">HT6 is 0.5 cun proximal to the radial side of the proximal border<br>of pisiform bone, 0.5 cun proximal to HT7, at the same level as<br>the distal border of the head of the ulna.</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">flexor carpi ulnaris<br>flexor digitorum superficialis<br>flexor digitorum profundus</td></tr>
<tr><td>nerve</td><td colspan="2">medial antebrachial cutaneous nerve<br>ulnar nerve<br>palmar branch of the ulnar nerve</td></tr>
<tr><td>blood<br>vessel</td><td colspan="2">median antebrachial vein<br>ulnar artery and vein</td></tr>
<tr><td>Method</td><td colspan="3">angle depth moxibustion ○</td></tr>
<tr><td>Specificity</td><td colspan="3">Xi-cleft point</td></tr>
<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">disease of the heart and throat, mental illness</td></tr>
<tr><td>HT7</td><td colspan="2">신문 Sinmun 神門 Shenmen</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">On the anteromedial aspect of the wrist, lateral border of the<br>flexor carpi ulnaris tendon, at the palmar wrist crease</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">flexor carpi ulnaris<br>flexor digitorum superficialis<br>flexor digitorum profundus<br>abductor digiti minimi of the hand</td></tr>
<tr><td>nerve</td><td colspan="2">medial antebrachial cutaneous nerve<br>ulnar nerve<br>palmar branch of the ulnar nerve</td></tr>
<tr><td>blood<br>vessel</td><td colspan="2">basilic vein<br>ulnar artery and vein</td></tr>
<tr><td>Method</td><td colspan="3">angle depth moxibustion ○<br>Caution Damage of the ulnar artery</td></tr>
<tr><td>Specificity</td><td colspan="3">Shu-stream point, Yuan-source point</td></tr>
<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">disease of the heart, mental illness</td></tr>
<tr><td>HT7</td><td colspan="2">소부 Sobu 少府 Shaofu</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">On the palm, between the 4th and 5th metacarpal bones, in the<br>depression proximal to the 5th metacarpophalangeal joint</td></tr>
<tr><td>Guide</td><td colspan="3">HT8 is located where the tip of the little finger rests when a fist<br>is made, at the same level as PC8.</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">flexor digitorum superficialis<br>opponens digiti minimi muscle<br>lumbrical muscle of hand<br>4th dorsal interosseous muscle of hand</td></tr>
<tr><td>nerve</td><td colspan="2">superficial branch of the ulnar nerve<br>common palmar digital branch of the ulnar nerve</td></tr>
<tr><td>blood<br>vessel</td><td colspan="2">superficial venous palmar arch<br>deep venous palmar arch</td></tr>
<tr><td>Method</td><td colspan="3">angle depth moxibustion ○<br>Caution Puncture towards the dorsal side from the palmar side.</td></tr>
<tr><td>Specificity</td><td colspan="3">Ying-spring point</td></tr>
<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">disease of the heart and thorax</td></tr>
<tr><td>HT9</td><td colspan="2">소충 Sochung 少衝 Shaochong</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">On the little finger, radial to the distal phalanx, 0.1 cun<br>proximal-lateral to the radial corner of the little fingernail</td></tr>
<tr><td>Guide</td><td colspan="3">HT8 is located where the tip of the little finger rests when a fist<br>is made, at the same level as PC8.</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">nail matrix</td></tr>
<tr><td>nerve</td><td colspan="2">proper palmar digital branch of the ulnar nerve<br>dorsal digital branch of the ulnar nerve</td></tr>
<tr><td>blood<br>vessel</td><td colspan="2">dorsal digital artery and vein<br>proper palmar digital artery<br>palmar digital vein</td></tr>
<tr><td>Method</td><td colspan="3">angle depth or bloodletting moxibustion ○<br>Caution Puncture towards the dorsal side from the palmar side.</td></tr>
<tr><td>Specificity</td><td colspan="3">Jing-well point</td></tr>
<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">disease of the heart and thorax, mental illness</td></tr>

# Small Intestine Meridian

![image](image_012.png)

<table>
<tr><th>1</th><th></th><th>The origin of the name</th><th></th></tr>
<tr><td>hand</td><td>手</td><td>the upper limb</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>taiyang</td><td>太陽</td><td></td><td>outside of the posterior aspect of the human body</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="4">small</td></tr>
<tr><td>intestine</td><td>小腸</td><td></td><td>the small intestine related meridian</td></tr>
<tr><td>2</td><td></td><td>The course of the SI meridian</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td></td><td>1. The little finger, SI meridian starts from the tip of the</td><td>abductor digiti minimi</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td></td><td>2. and it travels upward along the ulnar side of</td><td>extensor carpi ulnaris</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>the lower and upper arm,</td><td></td><td>triceps brachii supraspinatus infraspinatus</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>3. and it reaches the shoulder joint,</td><td></td><td>teres major teres minor</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="4">latissimus dorsi</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="4">levator scapulae</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>4. and it passes through the neck,</td><td></td><td>scalene sternocleidomastoid</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>5. and it reaches the cheeks and ears.</td><td></td><td>temporalis</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="4">132</td></tr>
</table>

<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">palmaris brevis<br>abductor digiti minimi<br>flexor digiti minimi brevis<br>tendon of the extensor carpi ulnaris</td></tr>

<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">disease of the head and posterior neck</td></tr>

<tr><td>Guide</td><td colspan="3">With the elbow slightly flexed, SI8 is located in the groove for the<br>ulnar nerve. It you tap the groove, you can feel a tingle all the<br>way to the little finger.</td></tr>

<tr><td>Specificity</td><td colspan="3">He-sea point</td></tr>
<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">disease of the head and posterior neck</td></tr>

<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">disease of the arm and shoulder</td></tr>

<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">disease of the thorax and lung</td></tr>

# Bladder Meridian

![image](image_013.png)

<table>
<tr><th>1</th><th></th><th>The origin of the name</th><th></th></tr>
<tr><td>foot</td><td>足</td><td>the lower limb</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>taiyang</td><td>太陽</td><td></td><td>outside of the posterior aspect of the human body</td></tr>
<tr><td>bladder</td><td>膀胱</td><td></td><td>the urinary bladder related meridian</td></tr>
<tr><td>2</td><td></td><td>The course of the BL meridian</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td></td><td>1. The BL meridian starts from the medial canthus,</td><td>orbicularis oculi</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="4">occipitofrontalis</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td></td><td>2. and it goes upward to the forehead and top of the head,</td><td>trapezius</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td></td><td>and downward along the posterior neck,</td><td>splenius capitis splenius cervicis</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="4">erector spinae</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td></td><td>3. and it travels along the vertebral column,</td><td>┌ multifidus</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td></td><td>(the inner 1 st line and the outer 2 nd line)</td><td>├ longissimus</td></tr>
</table>

└ iliocostalis

4. and it passes the buttock and the thigh,
and it comes out of the popliteal fossa,

gluteus maximus

biceps femoris

gastrocnemius

soleus

calcaneal tendon

fibularis longus

fibularis brevis

5. and it passes the posterior border
of the lateral malleolus along the leg,

abductor digiti minimi

flexor digiti minimi

6. and it reaches the lateral end of the little toe.

<tr><td>Specificity</td><td colspan="3">He-sea point, Lower He-sea point of the bladder, Command point</td></tr>
<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">disease of the lumbar region and lower limb, urogenital disease</td></tr>
<tr><td>BL55</td><td colspan="2">합양 Habyang 合陽 Heyang</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">On the posterior aspect of the leg, between the lateral and medial<br>head of the gastrocnemius, 2 cun distal to the popliteal crease</td></tr>
<tr><td>Guide</td><td colspan="3">BL55 is on the line connecting BL40 with BL57, 2 cun distal to<br>BL40.</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">gastrocnemius<br>plantaris</td></tr>
<tr><td>nerve</td><td colspan="2">posterior femoral cutaneous nerve<br>medial sural cutaneous nerve<br>tibial nerve</td></tr>
<tr><td>blood<br>vessel</td><td colspan="2">small saphenous vein<br>posterior tibial artery and vein</td></tr>
<tr><td>Method</td><td colspan="3">angle depth moxibustion ○</td></tr>
<tr><td>Specificity</td><td colspan="3">disease of the anus, lumbar region and lower limb,<br>gynecological disease</td></tr>
<tr><td>BL56</td><td colspan="2">승근 Seunggeun 承筋 Chengjin</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">On the posterior aspect of the leg, between the two bellies of the<br>gastrocnemius, 5 cun distal to the popliteal crease</td></tr>
<tr><td>Guide</td><td colspan="3">BL56 is at the midpoint of the line connecting BL55 and BL57,<br>center of the belly of the gastrocnemius.</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">gastrocnemius<br>soleus</td></tr>
<tr><td>nerve</td><td colspan="2">medial sural cutaneous nerve<br>tibial nerve</td></tr>
<tr><td>blood<br>vessel</td><td colspan="2">small saphenous vein<br>posterior tibial artery and vein<br>fibular artery and vein</td></tr>
<tr><td>Method</td><td colspan="3">angle depth moxibustion ○</td></tr>
<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">disease of the anus, lumbar region and lower limb</td></tr>
<tr><td>BL57</td><td colspan="2">승산 Seungsan 承山 Chengshan</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">On the posterior aspect of the leg, in the depression where the two<br>bellies of the gastrocnemius and the calcaneal tendon are<br>connected</td></tr>
<tr><td>Guide</td><td colspan="3">With the leg stretched or the heel up, BL57 is in the sharp angled<br>depression inferior to the bellies of the gastrocnemius, lambda<br>shape formed by the separation of the medial and lateral heads of<br>the gastrocnemius.</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">gastrocnemius<br>soleus<br>flexor hallucis longus<br>tibialis posterior</td></tr>
<tr><td>nerve</td><td colspan="2">medial sural cutaneous nerve<br>lateral sural cutaneous nerve<br>tibial nerve</td></tr>
<tr><td>blood<br>vessel</td><td colspan="2">small saphenous vein<br>posterior tibial artery and vein</td></tr>
<tr><td>Method</td><td colspan="3">angle depth moxibustion ○</td></tr>
<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">disease of the anus, lumbar region and lower limb</td></tr>
<tr><td>BL58</td><td colspan="2">비양 Biyang 飛揚 Feiyang</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">On the posterolateral aspect of the leg, between the inferior border<br>of the lateral head of the gastrocnemius and the calcaneal tendon,<br>at the same level as 7 cun proximal to BL60</td></tr>
<tr><td>Guide</td><td colspan="3">BL58 is located 1 cun lateral and distal to BL57, proximal to<br>BL60.</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">lateral head of the gastrocnemius<br>soleus<br>flexor hallucis longus</td></tr>
<tr><td>nerve</td><td colspan="2">lateral sural cutaneous nerve<br>tibial nerve</td></tr>
<tr><td>blood<br>vessel</td><td colspan="2">small saphenous vein<br>posterior tibial artery and vein<br>fibular artery and vein</td></tr>
<tr><td>Method</td><td colspan="3">angle depth moxibustion ○</td></tr>
<tr><td>Specificity</td><td colspan="3">Luo-connecting point</td></tr>
<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">disease of the head, face, lumbar region and lower limb</td></tr>
<tr><td>BL59</td><td colspan="2">부양 Buyang 跗陽 Fuyang</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">On the posterolateral aspect of the leg, between the fibula and the<br>calcaneal tendon, 3 cun proximal to BL60</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">fibularis brevis<br>fibularis longus<br>flexor hallucis longus</td></tr>
<tr><td>nerve</td><td colspan="2">lateral sural cutaneous nerve<br>sural nerve<br>superficial fibular nerve</td></tr>
<tr><td>blood<br>vessel</td><td colspan="2">small saphenous vein<br>fibular artery and vein</td></tr>
<tr><td>Method</td><td colspan="3">angle depth moxibustion ○</td></tr>
<tr><td>Specificity</td><td colspan="3">Xi-cleft point of the Yangqiao meridian</td></tr>
<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">disease of the lumbar region and lower limb</td></tr>
<tr><td>BL60</td><td colspan="2">곤륜 Gollyun 崑崙 Kunlun</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">On the postero-lateral aspect of the ankle, in the depression<br>between the prominence of the lateral malleolus and the calcaneal<br>tendon</td></tr>
<tr><td>Guide</td><td colspan="3">BL60 is at the midpoint of the horizontal line connecting the<br>lateral malleolus and the calcaneal tendon.</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">calcaneal tendon<br>fibularis longus<br>fibularis brevis</td></tr>
<tr><td>nerve</td><td colspan="2">sural nerve<br>superficial fibular nerve</td></tr>
<tr><td>blood<br>vessel</td><td colspan="2">small saphenous vein<br>fibular artery and vein</td></tr>
<tr><td>Method</td><td colspan="3">angle or depth moxibustion ○<br>Caution No acupuncture for pregnant women</td></tr>
<tr><td>Specificity</td><td colspan="3">Jing-river point</td></tr>
<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">disease of the head, posterior neck, lumbar region and lower limb</td></tr>
<tr><td>BL61</td><td colspan="2">복삼 Boksam 僕參 Pucan</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">On the lateral aspect of the foot, distal to BL60, at the border<br>between the red and white flesh</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">calcaneal tendon</td></tr>
<tr><td>nerve</td><td colspan="2">lateral calcaneal branch of the sural nerve</td></tr>
<tr><td>blood<br>vessel</td><td colspan="2">small saphenous vein<br>fibular artery and vein<br>calcaneal branch of the fibular artery</td></tr>
<tr><td>Method</td><td colspan="3">angle depth moxibustion ○</td></tr>
<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">disease of the lumbar region and lower limb</td></tr>
<tr><td>BL62</td><td colspan="2">신맥 Sinmaek 申脈 Shenmai</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">On the lateral aspect of the foot, directly inferior to the<br>prominence of the lateral malleolus, in the depression between the<br>inferior border of the lateral malleolus and the calcaneus</td></tr>
<tr><td>Guide</td><td colspan="3">BL62 is in the depression distal to the inferior border of the<br>lateral malleolus.</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">superior fibular retinaculum<br>tendon of the fibularis brevis<br>tendon of the fibularis longus<br>latreral talocalcaneal ligament</td></tr>
<tr><td>nerve</td><td colspan="2">lateral calcaneal branch of the sural nerve</td></tr>
<tr><td>blood<br>vessel</td><td colspan="2">small saphenous vein<br>fibular artery and vein<br>anterolateral malleolar artery and vein</td></tr>
<tr><td>Method</td><td colspan="3">angle depth moxibustion ○</td></tr>
<tr><td>Specificity</td><td colspan="3">Confluent point (the Yangqiao meridian)</td></tr>
<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">disease of the head, lumbar region and lower limb</td></tr>
<tr><td>BL63</td><td colspan="2">금문 Geummun 金門 Jinmen</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">On the dorsum of the foot, distal to the anterior border of the<br>lateral malleolus, posterior to the tuberosity of the 5th metatarsal<br>bone, in the depression distal to the cuboid</td></tr>
<tr><td>Guide</td><td colspan="3">The tuberosity of the 5th metatarsal bone is generally at the<br>midpoint of the line connecting the heel and the 5th<br>metatarsophalangeal joint.</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">extensor digitorum brevis<br>dorsal calcaneocuboid ligament<br>tendon of the fibularis brevis<br>tendon of the fibularis longus</td></tr>
<tr><td>nerve</td><td colspan="2">lateral dorsal cutaneous nerve</td></tr>
<tr><td>blood<br>vessel</td><td colspan="2">lateral marginal vein (from the small saphenous vein)<br>lateral malleolar network<br>lateral plantar artery and vein</td></tr>
<tr><td>Method</td><td colspan="3">angle depth moxibustion ○</td></tr>
<tr><td>Specificity</td><td colspan="3">Xi-cleft point</td></tr>
<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">disease of the head, lumbar region and lower limb</td></tr>
<tr><td>BL64</td><td colspan="2">경골 Gyeonggol 京骨 Jinggu</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">On the lateral aspect of the foot, distal to the tuberosity of the 5th<br>metatarsal bone, at the border between the red and white flesh</td></tr>
<tr><td>Guide</td><td colspan="3">The tuberosity of the 5th metatarsal bone is generally at the<br>midpoint of the line connecting the heel and the 5th<br>metatarsophalangeal joint.</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">flexor digiti minimi brevis<br>abductor digiti minimi</td></tr>
<tr><td>nerve</td><td colspan="2">lateral dorsal cutaneous nerve<br>lateral plantar nerve</td></tr>
<tr><td>blood<br>vessel</td><td colspan="2">lateral marginal vein (from the small saphenous vein)<br>lateral plantar artery and vein</td></tr>
<tr><td>Method</td><td colspan="3">angle depth moxibustion ○</td></tr>
<tr><td>Specificity</td><td colspan="3">Yuan-source point</td></tr>
<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">disease of the head, posterior neck, lumbar region and lower limb</td></tr>
<tr><td>BL65</td><td colspan="2">속골 Sokgol 束骨 Shugu</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">On the lateral aspect of the foot, in the depression proximal to the<br>5th metatarsophalangeal joint, at the border between the red and<br>white flesh</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">abductor digiti minimi<br>flexor digiti minimi brevis<br>plantar aponeurosis</td></tr>
<tr><td>nerve</td><td colspan="2">lateral dorsal cutaneous nerve<br>lateral plantar nerve</td></tr>
<tr><td>blood<br>vessel</td><td colspan="2">dorsal metatarsal artery and vein<br>lateral plantar artery and vein</td></tr>
<tr><td>Method</td><td colspan="3">angle depth moxibustion ○</td></tr>
<tr><td>Specificity</td><td colspan="3">Shu-stream point</td></tr>
<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">disease of the head and posterior neck</td></tr>
<tr><td>BL66</td><td colspan="2">족통곡 Joktonggok 足通谷 Zutonggu</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">On the little toe, in the depression distal to the 5th<br>metatarsophalangeal joint, at the border between the red and<br>white flesh</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">tendon of the abductor digiti minimi<br>tendon of the flexor digiti minimi brevis</td></tr>
<tr><td>nerve</td><td colspan="2">lateral dorsal cutaneous nerve<br>lateral plantar nerve</td></tr>
<tr><td>blood<br>vessel</td><td colspan="2">dorsal metatarsal artery and vein<br>plantar digital artery and vein</td></tr>
<tr><td>Method</td><td colspan="3">angle depth moxibustion ○</td></tr>
<tr><td>Specificity</td><td colspan="3">Ying-spring point</td></tr>
<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">disease of the head and posterior neck</td></tr>
<tr><td>BL67</td><td colspan="2">지음 Jieum 至陰 Zhiyin</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">On the little toe, lateral to the distal phalanx, 0.1 cun proximal to<br>the lateral corner of the little toenail</td></tr>
<tr><td>Guide</td><td colspan="3">BL67 is at the intersection of the vertical line of the lateral border<br>and the horizontal line of the base of the little toenail.</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">nail matrix</td></tr>
<tr><td>nerve</td><td colspan="2">dorsal digital nerve<br>proper plantar digital branch<br>of the lateral plantar nerve</td></tr>
<tr><td>blood<br>vessel</td><td colspan="2">dorsal digital artery and vein<br>plantar digital artery and vein</td></tr>
<tr><td>Method</td><td colspan="3">angle depth moxibustion ○</td></tr>
<tr><td>Specificity</td><td colspan="3">Jing-well point</td></tr>
<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">disease of the head and face, gynecological disease</td></tr>

# 足

# KI

# Kidney Meridian

![image](image_014.png)

| 1 |  | The origin of the name |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| foot | 足 | the lower limb |  |
| shaoyin | 少陰 |  | inside of the posterior aspect of the human body |
| kidney | 腎 |  | the kidney related meridian |
| 2 |  | The course of the KI meridian |  |

1. The KI meridian starts from the plantar side
of the little toe, passes the center of the sole,

plantar aponeurosis

flexor digitorum longus

<table>
<tr><th></th><th></th><th>and malleolus, reaches the navicular bone and medial</th><th>flexor hallucis longus</th></tr>
<tr><td colspan="4">calcaneal tendon</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td></td><td>2. and it goes along the inside of the posterior</td><td>soleus</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>aspect of the leg,</td><td></td><td>tendons of the semitendinous and semimembranous rectus abdominis external oblique abdominis</td></tr>
</table>

3. and it travels upward through the abdomen,
internal oblique abdominis

transversus abdominis

pectoralis major

external intercostalis

internal intercostalis

4. and it reaches the thorax.

<table>
<tr><th>KI1</th><th colspan="2">용천 Yongcheon 湧泉 Yongquan</th><th></th></tr>
<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">On the sole of the foot, in the deepest depression of the sole with<br>the toes flexed</td></tr>
<tr><td>Guide</td><td colspan="3">With the toes flexed, KI1 is located approximately in the<br>depression at the junction of the anterior 1/3 and the posterior<br>2/3 of the line connecting the heel and the web margin between<br>the bases of the second and third toes.</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">plantar aponeurosis<br>flexor digitorum brevis<br>flexor digitorum longus<br>lumbricals pedis<br>oblique head of the adductor hallucis</td></tr>
<tr><td>nerve</td><td colspan="2">common plantar digital branch<br>of the medial plantar nerve<br>deep branch of the lateral plantar nerve</td></tr>
<tr><td>blood<br>vessel</td><td colspan="2">plantar metatarsal artery and vein<br>plantar arterial arch<br>plantar venous arch</td></tr>
<tr><td>Method</td><td colspan="3">angle depth moxibustion ○</td></tr>
<tr><td>Specificity</td><td colspan="3">Jing-well point</td></tr>
<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">fever, urogenital disease, disease of the throat and back</td></tr>
<tr><td>KI2</td><td colspan="2">연곡 Yeongok 然谷 Rangu</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">On the medial aspect of the foot, inferior to the tuberosity of the<br>navicular, at the border between the red and white flesh</td></tr>
<tr><td>Guide</td><td colspan="3">KI2 is located in the depression anterior inferior to the medial<br>malleolus which is below the navicular.</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">tendon of the tibialis posterior<br>flexor hallucis brevis<br>tendon of the flexor hallucis longus<br>tendon of the flexor digitorum longus<br>abductor hallucis</td></tr>
<tr><td>nerve</td><td colspan="2">medial plantar nerve<br>medial crural cutaneous branch of the saphenous nerve</td></tr>
<tr><td>blood<br>vessel</td><td colspan="2">medial tarsal artery<br>medial marginal vein<br>dorsal venous network<br>medial plantar artery and vein</td></tr>
<tr><td>Method</td><td colspan="3">angle depth or bloodletting moxibustion ○</td></tr>
<tr><td>Specificity</td><td colspan="3">Ying-spring point, Entry point of the Yinqiao meridian</td></tr>
<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">urogenital disease, disease of the throat</td></tr>
<tr><td>KI3</td><td colspan="2">태계 Taegye 太谿 Taixi</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">On the postero-medial aspect of the ankle, in the depression<br>between the prominence of the medial malleolus and the calcaneal<br>tendon</td></tr>
<tr><td>Guide</td><td colspan="3">KI3 is located between the tendons of the flexor hallucis longus<br>and flexor digitorum longus.</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">tendon of the tibialis posterior<br>tendon of the flexor digitorum longus<br>tendon of the flexor hallucis longus</td></tr>
<tr><td>nerve</td><td colspan="2">tibial nerve<br>medial crural cutaneous branch of the saphenous nerve</td></tr>
<tr><td>blood<br>vessel</td><td colspan="2">posterior tibial artery and vein</td></tr>
<tr><td>Method</td><td colspan="3">angle depth moxibustion ○</td></tr>
<tr><td>Specificity</td><td colspan="3">Shu-stream point, Yuan-source point</td></tr>
<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">urogenital disease, disease of the large intestine and throat</td></tr>
<tr><td>KI4</td><td colspan="2">대종 Daejong 大鍾 Dazhong</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">On the medial aspect of the foot, postero-inferior to the medial<br>malleolus, superior to the calcaneus, in the depression anterior to<br>the medial attachment of the calcaneal tendon</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">tendon of the flexor hallucis longus<br>tendon of the plantaris<br>calcaneal tendon</td></tr>
<tr><td>nerve</td><td colspan="2">medial calcaneal branch of the tibial nerve</td></tr>
<tr><td>blood<br>vessel</td><td colspan="2">calcaneal branch of the posterior tibial artery and vein</td></tr>
<tr><td>Method</td><td colspan="3">angle depth moxibustion ○</td></tr>
<tr><td>Specificity</td><td colspan="3">Luo-connecting point</td></tr>
<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">urogenital disease, mental disease, disease of the throat and back</td></tr>
<tr><td>KI5</td><td colspan="2">수천 Sucheon 水泉 Shuiquan</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">On the medial aspect of the foot, 1 cun inferior to KI3, in the<br>depression anterior to the calcaneal tuberosity</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">flexor hallucis longus</td></tr>
<tr><td>nerve</td><td colspan="2">medial calcaneal branch of the tibial nerve</td></tr>
<tr><td>blood<br>vessel</td><td colspan="2">calcaneal branch of the posterior tibial artery and vein</td></tr>

# Pericardium Meridian

![image](image_015.png)

| 1 |  | The origin of the name |
| --- | --- | --- |
| hand | 手 | the upper limb |
| jueyin | 厥陰 | inside of the lateral aspect of the human body |
| pericardium | 心包 | the pericardium related meridian |
| 2 |  | The course of the PC meridian |

1. The PC meridian originates from the heart
and comes out through the axillary,

2. and it goes down along the inside
of the lateral aspect of the upper arm,

between the long and short head

of the biceps brachii

flexor carpi radialis

palmaris longus

flexor digitorum superficialis

3. and it goes between the muscles
of the forearm,

4. and it is revealed at the palm
and the middle finger of the hand.

# Triple Energizer Meridian

1. The TE meridian starts
at the tip of the ring finger

extensor carpi ulnaris

extensor pollicis longus

extensor digitorum

extensor digiti minimi

2. and it is revealed
between the radius and ulna,

triceps brachii

supraspinatus

levator scapulae

3. and it travels along the lateral side of the arm
and passes through the shoulder,

<table>
<tr><th></th><th>4. and it goes upward along the neck,</th><th></th><th>sternocleidomastoid</th></tr>
<tr><td></td><td></td><td>5. and it reaches the ear and the temporal region.</td><td>temporalis</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="4">272</td></tr>
</table>

### 2	The course of the GB meridian

| 1. The GB meridian starts from the lateral canthus, | orbicularis oculi |
| --- | --- |
| and it goes upward to the hairline and posterior | occipitofrontalis |
| to the ears, | temporalis trapezius splenius capitis |

2. and it passes the posterior neck and shoulder,
splenius cervicis

levator scapulae

serratus anterior

<table>
<tr><th>3. and it goes down along the axillary and the ribs,</th><th>external intercostalis interal intercostalis</th></tr>
<tr><td colspan="2">gluteus</td></tr>
<tr><td>4. and it passes through the inguinal canal</td><td>piriformis</td></tr>
<tr><td>and travels along the lateral side</td><td>tensor fascia latae</td></tr>
<tr><td>of the thigh and the knee from the hip joint,</td><td>iliotibial tract vastus lateralis</td></tr>
</table>

5. and it goes anterior to the fibula and lateral
malleolus,

6. and it reaches between the 4th and 5th toes along
the dorsal side of the foot.

fibularis longus

fibularis brevis

extensor digitorum longus

extensor digitorum brevis

# 足

# LR

# Liver Meridian

![image](image_018.png)

| 1 |  | The origin of the name |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| foot | 足 | the lower limb |  |
| jueyin | 厥陰 |  | inside of the lateral aspect of the human body |
| liver | 肝 |  | the liver related meridian |
| 2 |  | The course of the LR meridian |  |

1. The LR meridian starts from the great toe,
passes the dorsal side of the foot and goes

anterior to the medial malleolus,

dorsal interosseous

tendon of the tibialis anterior

tibialis posterior

flexor digitorum longus

semimembranous

semitendinous

2. and it travels along the medial side of the
leg and crosses the SP meridian to reach

the popliteal fossa,

sartorius

gracilis

adductor group

pectineus

3. and it goes along the medial side of the
thigh and enters the inguinal region,

external oblique abdominis

external intercostalis

internal intercostalis

4. and it penetrates the diaphragm to reach
the flank.

### 3	The acupoints of the LR meridian

<tr><td>LR12</td><td colspan="2">급맥 Geummaek 急脈 Jimai</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">In the groin region, at the same level as the superior border of the<br>pubic symphysis, 2.5 cun lateral to the anterior midline</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">pectineus<br>obturator externus</td></tr>
<tr><td>nerve</td><td colspan="2">ilioinguinal nerve<br>anterior branch of the obturator nerve<br>anterior cutaneous branch of the femoral nerve<br>muscular branch of the femoral nerve</td></tr>
<tr><td>blood<br>vessel</td><td colspan="2">great saphenous vein<br>external pudendal artery and vein<br>femoral artery and vein<br>superficial epigastric artery and vein</td></tr>
<tr><td>Method</td><td colspan="3">angle depth moxibustion ○<br>Caution Damage to the blood vessels</td></tr>
<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">urogenital disease</td></tr>
<tr><td>LR13</td><td colspan="2">장문 Jangmun 章門 Zhangmen</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">On the lateral abdomen, inferior to the free extremity of the 11th<br>rib</td></tr>
<tr><td>Guide</td><td colspan="3">Lying on the side with the shoulder flexed, the free extremity of<br>the 11th rib can be palpated below the inferior border of the costal<br>arch.</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">external oblique abdominis<br>internal oblique abdominis<br>transversus abdominis</td></tr>
<tr><td>nerve</td><td colspan="2">10th-11th intercostal nerve</td></tr>
<tr><td>blood<br>vessel</td><td colspan="2">10th-11th posterior intercostal artery and vein<br>thoracoepigastric vein</td></tr>
<tr><td>Method</td><td colspan="3">angle depth moxibustion ○</td></tr>
<tr><td>Specificity</td><td colspan="3">Front-Mu point of the spleen</td></tr>
<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">disease of the digestive system</td></tr>
<tr><td>LR14</td><td colspan="2">기문 Gimun 期門 Qimen</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>Location</td><td colspan="3">In the anterior thoracic region, in the 6th intercostal space, 4 cun<br>lateral to the anterior midline</td></tr>
<tr><td>Guide</td><td colspan="3">LR14 is located at the intersection of the midclavicular line and<br>the 6th intercostal space, inferior to the center of the nipple.</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Anatomy</td><td>muscle</td><td colspan="2">pectoralis major<br>external oblique abdominis<br>external intercostalis<br>internal intercostalis</td></tr>

<tr><td>Specificity</td><td colspan="3">Front-Mu point of the liver</td></tr>
<tr><td>Usage</td><td colspan="3">disease of the digestive system, thorax and flank</td></tr>

# 任

# 脈

# CV

# Conception Vessel

![image](image_019.png)

任

• in charge of pregnancy

• related to menstruation

Conception

### 2	The course of the CV meridian

1. The CV meridian starts from the perineal region,
goes upward to the pubic symphysis,

perineal body

levator ani

linea alba

transversalis fascia

2. and it travels along the anterior median line,
sternohyoid

mylohyoid

mentalis

obicularis oris

3. and it reaches to the neck and face.

# 督

# 脈

# GV

# Governor Vessel

![image](image_020.png)

督

• to govern, control and coordinate

• related to the central nervous system

(the brain and spinal cord)

Governor

### 2	The course of the GV meridian

1. The GV meridian originates from the lower abdomen,
reaches the anus,

levator ani

trapezius

thoracolumbar fascia

<table>
<tr><th>2. and it travels over the posterior median line</th><th>supraspinous ligament</th></tr>
<tr><td>along the vertebral column,</td><td>interspinous ligament interspinales</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="2">multifidus</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="2">nuchal ligament</td></tr>
<tr><td>3. and it passes the posterior neck to the top of the head,</td><td>epicranial aponeurosis occipitofrontalis</td></tr>
<tr><td>4. and it reaches the face, philtrum and mouth.</td><td>orbicularis oris</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="2">388</td></tr>
</table>
